+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
+// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
+struct PricingCoefficients {
+    int type;
+    double strike;
+    double spot;
+    double drift;
+    double volatilityTime;
+    double dividendDiscount;
+    double discount;
+};
+
+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];
+
+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];
+        table[i] = {
+            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
+            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)

-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingCoefficients& option,
+                                              double factor) {
+    if (option.volatilityTime <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    // Scale both inputs before taking their ratio, just as in the original.
+    // Scaling a precomputed price would change rounding and cancellation.
+    const double S = option.spot * factor;
+    const double K = option.strike * factor;
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volatilityTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.volatilityTime;
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1)
+             - K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);
+    }
+    return K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2)
+         - S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t numOptions, size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 && collectCount != 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const Range range = partition(numOptions, r, ranks);
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
+    }
+}
+

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import math
import os
import re
import subprocess

reference = '/tmp/black_scholes_reference_r3'
hybrid = '/tmp/black_scholes_build_r3/black_scholes'

def run(command, env=None, expected=0):
    result = subprocess.run(command, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env, timeout=60)
    assert result.returncode == expected, (command, result.returncode, result.stdout, result.stderr)
    return result.stdout

def results(output):
    match = re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===\\n(.*?)=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    assert match, output
    lines = match.group(1).splitlines()
    return {line.split(': ', 1)[0]: line.split(': ', 1)[1] for line in lines}

def compare(expected, actual):
    a, b = results(expected), results(actual)
    assert a.keys() == b.keys(), (a, b)
    max_relative = 0.0
    for key in a:
        if key == 'Hash':
            continue
        if key in ('Name', 'Elements'):
            assert a[key] == b[key], (key, a[key], b[key])
        else:
            x, y = float(a[key]), float(b[key])
            assert math.isclose(x, y, rel_tol=2e-11, abs_tol=1e-10), (key, x, y)
            max_relative = max(max_relative, abs(x - y) / max(abs(x), 1e-100))
    return max_relative

for count in (0, 1, 2, 7, 10, 37, 10000):
    before = run([reference, '-n', str(count), '-v', '-r'])
    after = run([hybrid, '-n', str(count), '-v', '-r'])
    error = compare(before, after)
    assert 'Validation: PASSED' in after
    print(f'Serial comparison n={count}: PASS (max relative difference {error:.3e})', flush=True)

for ranks, count in ((2, 1), (3, 37), (4, 100003), (6, 3)):
    after = run(['mpirun', '--oversubscribe', '--bind-to', 'none', '-np', str(ranks), hybrid, '-n', str(count), '-v', '-r'])
    single = run([hybrid, '-n', str(count), '-v', '-r'])
    assert results(single) == results(after), (ranks, count, results(single), results(after))
    assert after.count('Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark') == 1
    print(f'MPI ranks={ranks}, n={count}: PASS (bitwise identical results and one output block)', flush=True)

restricted_env = dict(os.environ, OMP_THREAD_LIMIT='1')
after = run([hybrid, '-n', '10000', '-r'], env=restricted_env)
single = run([hybrid, '-n', '10000', '-r'])
assert results(after) == results(single)
print('OMP_THREAD_LIMIT=1: PASS (all GPU tasks executed)', flush=True)

for args, code in ((['-h'], 0), (['-n', '-1'], 1), (['-n', 'abc'], 1), (['-n'], 1), (['--bad'], 1)):
    output = run(['mpirun', '--bind-to', 'none', '-np', '2', hybrid] + args, expected=code)
    if args == ['-h']:
        assert output.count('Usage:') == 1
    print(f'Collective command-line handling {args}: PASS', flush=True)

run([hybrid, '-n', '1'], env=dict(os.environ, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=''), expected=1)
print('No visible GPU: PASS (required CUDA execution fails explicitly)', flush=True)

+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
+// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
+struct PricingCoefficients {
+    int type;
+    double strike;
+    double spot;
+    double drift;
+    double volatilityTime;
+    double dividendDiscount;
+    double discount;
+};
+
+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];

+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];
+        table[i] = {
+            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
+            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)

+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingCoefficients& option,
+                                              double factor) {
+    if (option.volatilityTime <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    // Scale both inputs before taking their ratio, just as in the original.
+    // Scaling a precomputed price would change rounding and cancellation.
+    const double S = option.spot * factor;
+    const double K = option.strike * factor;
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volatilityTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.volatilityTime;
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1)
+             - K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);
+    }
+    return K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2)
+         - S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t numOptions, size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 && collectCount != 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const Range range = partition(numOptions, r, ranks);
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
+    }
+}
+

+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
+// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
+struct PricingCoefficients {
+    int type;
+    double strike;
+    double spot;
+    double drift;
+    double volatilityTime;
+    double dividendDiscount;
+    double discount;
+};
+
+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];
+
+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];
+        table[i] = {
+            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
+            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)
+        };
     }
+    return table;

-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingCoefficients& option,
+                                              double factor) {
+    if (option.volatilityTime <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    // Scale both inputs before taking their ratio, just as in the original.
+    // Scaling a precomputed price would change rounding and cancellation.
+    const double S = option.spot * factor;
+    const double K = option.strike * factor;
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volatilityTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.volatilityTime;
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1)
+             - K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);
+    }
+    return K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2)
+         - S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t numOptions, size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 && collectCount != 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const Range range = partition(numOptions, r, ranks);
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
+    }
+}
+

+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
+// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
+struct PricingCoefficients {
+    int type;
+    double strike;
+    double spot;
+    double drift;
+    double volatilityTime;
+    double dividendDiscount;
+    double discount;
+};
+
+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];
+
+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];
+        table[i] = {
+            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
+            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)
+        };
     }
+    return table;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingCoefficients& option,
+                                              double factor) {
+    if (option.volatilityTime <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    // Scale both inputs before taking their ratio, just as in the original.
+    // Scaling a precomputed price would change rounding and cancellation.
+    const double S = option.spot * factor;
+    const double K = option.strike * factor;
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volatilityTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.volatilityTime;
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1)
+             - K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);
+    }
+    return K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2)
+         - S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t numOptions, size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 && collectCount != 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const Range range = partition(numOptions, r, ranks);
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
+    }
+}
+

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }

+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

-    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
-    options.resize(numOptions);
-

-        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));

-        options[i].strike *= factor;
+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }
 }

#define main benchmarkEntry
#include \"/home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/black-scholes_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r3/black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp\"
#undef main
inline double referenceNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}


double referencePrice(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = referenceNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = referenceNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * referenceNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * referenceNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}


int main() {
    int devices=0;
    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices), \"cudaGetDeviceCount\");
    const size_t first=101;
    const size_t count=static_cast<size_t>(devices)*3*GpuWorker::chunkOptions+37;
    std::vector<double> prices(count);
    std::vector<GpuWorker> workers(devices);
    for(int i=0;i<devices;++i) {
        workers[i].deviceId=i;
        workers[i].range=partition(count,i,devices);
        workers[i].range.first+=first;
    }
    const auto table=prepareCoefficients();
    onGpus(workers,0,[&](GpuWorker& worker){worker.initialize(table,first+count);});
    onGpus(workers,0,[&](GpuWorker& worker){worker.price(prices.data(),first,first+count);});
    onGpus(workers,0,[](GpuWorker& worker){worker.release();});
    constexpr auto options=getTestOptions();
    double maxRelative=0.0,maxAbsolute=0.0;
    size_t failures=0;
    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(8) reduction(max:maxRelative,maxAbsolute) reduction(+:failures)
    for(size_t i=0;i<count;++i) {
        const size_t index=first+i;
        auto input=options[index%options.size()];
        const double factor=1.0+0.1*(index/static_cast<double>(options.size()));
        input.spot*=factor;
        input.strike*=factor;
        const double expected=referencePrice(input);
        const double difference=fabs(prices[i]-expected);
        maxAbsolute=std::max(maxAbsolute,difference);
        maxRelative=std::max(maxRelative,difference/std::max(fabs(expected),1e-100));
        if("'!std::isfinite(prices[i]) || difference>1e-9+1e-11*fabs(expected)) {
            ++failures;
        }
    }
    printf("Full-array reference comparison: %zu prices, %d GPUs, nonzero global offset, four chunks per GPU'"\\n\",count,devices);
    printf(\"Failures: %zu; maximum relative difference: %.3e; maximum absolute difference: %.3e\\n\",failures,maxRelative,maxAbsolute);
    return failures?1:0;
}

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {

+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }
 }
