    19	#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
    20	#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
    21	#endif
    22	
    23	enum OptionType {
    24	    CALL = 0,
    25	    PUT = 1
    26	};
    27	
    28	struct OptionInput {
    29	    int type;
    30	    double strike;
    31	    double spot;
    32	    double q;
    33	    double r;
    34	    double t;
    35	    double vol;
    36	    double value;
    37	    double tol;
    38	};
    39	
    40	inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    41	    return {{
    42	        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
    43	        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
    44	        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
    45	        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
    46	        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
    47	        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
    48	        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    49	    }};
    50	}
    51	
    52	// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
    53	// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
    54	struct PricingCoefficients {
    55	    int type;
    56	    double strike;
    57	    double spot;
    58	    double drift;
    59	    double volatilityTime;
    60	    double dividendDiscount;
    61	    double discount;
    62	};
    63	
    64	using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
    65	__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];
    66	
    67	CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
    68	    CoefficientTable table{};
    69	    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
    70	    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
    71	        const auto& option = options[i];
    72	        table[i] = {
    73	            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
    74	            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
    75	            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
    76	            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)
    77	        };
    78	    }
    79	    return table;
    80	}
    81	
    82	__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) {
    83	    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
    84	}
    85	
    86	__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingCoefficients& option,
    87	                                              double factor) {
    88	    if (option.volatilityTime <= 0.0) {
    89	        return 0.0;
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    // Scale both inputs before taking their ratio, just as in the original.
    93	    // Scaling a precomputed price would change rounding and cancellation.
    94	    const double S = option.spot * factor;
    95	    const double K = option.strike * factor;
    96	    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volatilityTime;
    97	    const double d2 = d1 - option.volatilityTime;
    98	    if (option.type == CALL) {
    99	        return S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1)
   100	             - K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);
   101	    }
   102	    return K * option.discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2)
   103	         - S * option.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
   104	}
   105	
   106	__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
   107	    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
   108	    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   109	         i < count; i += stride) {
   110	        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
   111	        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
   112	        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
   113	    }
   114	}
   115	
   116	void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
   117	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
   118	        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
   119	    }
   120	}
   121	
   122	void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
   123	    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   124	        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
   125	        int length = 0;
   126	        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
   127	        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
   128	    }
   129	}
   130	
   131	struct Range {
   132	    size_t first;
   133	    size_t count;
   134	};
   135	
   136	Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
   137	    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
   138	    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
   139	    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
   140	            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
   141	}
   142	
   143	Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
   144	    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
   145	    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
   146	    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
   147	    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
   148	    return {first, end - first};
   149	}
   150	
   151	// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
   152	// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
   153	// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
   154	struct GpuWorker {
   155	    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
   156	    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
   157	
   158	    struct Buffer {
   159	        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   160	        double* device = nullptr;
   161	        double* host = nullptr;
   162	        size_t hostOffset = 0;
   163	        size_t copyCount = 0;
   164	        bool pending = false;
   165	    };
   166	
   167	    int deviceId = 0;
   168	    Range range{};
   169	    size_t capacity = 0;
   170	    int slots = 0;
   171	    int maxBlocks = 0;
   172	    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
   173	
   174	    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
   175	        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
   176	        if (range.count == 0) {
   177	            return;
   178	        }
   179	        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
   180	        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
   181	        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
   182	        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
   183	        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
   184	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
   185	                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
   186	        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
   187	            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
   188	            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
   189	                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
   190	            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
   191	                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
   192	            if (range.first < collectCount) {
   193	                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
   194	                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
   198	        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
   199	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
   200	        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
   201	    }
   202	
   203	    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
   204	        if (buffer.pending) {
   205	            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
   206	            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
   207	                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
   208	                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
   209	            }
   210	            buffer.pending = false;
   211	        }
   212	    }
   213	
   214	    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
   215	        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
   216	        int slot = 0;
   217	        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
   218	            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
   219	            finish(buffer, output);
   220	            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
   221	            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
   222	            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
   223	                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
   224	            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
   225	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
   226	            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
   227	            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
   228	                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
   229	                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
   230	                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
   231	                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
   232	                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
   233	            }
   234	            buffer.pending = true;
   235	            offset += count;
   236	            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
   237	        }
   238	        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
   239	            finish(buffers[i], output);
   240	        }
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    void release() {
   244	        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
   245	        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
   246	            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
   247	            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
   248	                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
   249	            }
   250	            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
   251	        }
   252	    }
   253	};
   254	
   255	template <typename Function>
   256	void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
   257	    int failed = 0;
   258	    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
   259	    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
   260	    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
   261	    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
   262	    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   263	        try {
   264	            action(workers[i]);
   265	        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   266	            #pragma omp critical
   267	            {
   268	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
   269	                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
   270	            }
   271	            failed = 1;
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	    if (failed) {
   275	        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
   276	    }
   277	}
   278	
   279	// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
   280	// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
   281	// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
   282	void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
   283	                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
   284	                   int rank, int ranks) {
   285	    if (collectCount == 0) {
   286	        return;
   287	    }
   288	    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
   289	    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
   290	    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
   291	                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
   292	    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
   293	    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
   294	    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   295	    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
   296	        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
   297	        if (rank == 0) {
   298	            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
   299	                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
   300	                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
   301	                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
   302	                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
   303	                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
   304	                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
   305	                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
   306	            }
   307	        }
   308	        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
   309	        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
   310	        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
   311	        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
   312	        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
   313	                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
   314	                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
   315	                 "MPI_Gatherv");
   316	        first = end;
   317	    }
   318	}
   319	
   320	bool validateResults(const std::vector<double>& results) {
   321	    bool allPassed = true;
   322	    const int numChecks = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), results.size()));
   323	    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
   324	
   325	    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
   326	    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
   327	        const double computed = results[i];
   328	        const double expected = options[i % options.size()].value;
   329	        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
   330	        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
   331	        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n",
   332	               i, computed, expected, relError);
   333	
   334	        // Preserve the original benchmark's relaxed validation.
   335	        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
   336	            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
   337	            allPassed = false;
   338	        }
   339	    }
   340	    return allPassed;
   341	}
   342	
   343	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   344	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   345	    printf("Options:\n");
   346	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
   347	    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
   348	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   349	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   350	}
   351	
   352	int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int ranks) {
   353	    // Parse once and broadcast, including early exits, to keep all ranks in
   354	    // the same collective sequence.
   355	    std::array<unsigned long long, 3> settings{{10000, 0, 0}};
   356	    int status = 0;
   357	    if (rank == 0) {
   358	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   359	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   360	                const char* argument = argv[++i];
   361	                char* end = nullptr;
   362	                errno = 0;
   363	                const unsigned long long count = std::strtoull(argument, &end, 10);
   364	                if (*argument == '-' || *argument == '\0' || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE
   365	                    || count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   366	                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid number of options: %s\n", argument);
   367	                    status = 1;
   368	                    break;
   369	                }
   370	                settings[0] = count;
   371	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   372	                settings[1] = 1;
   373	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   374	                settings[2] = 1;
   375	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   376	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   377	                status = -1;
   378	                break;
   379	            } else {
   380	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   381	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   382	                status = 1;
   383	                break;
   384	            }
   385	        }
   386	    }
   387	    checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Bcast");
   388	    if (status != 0) {
   389	        return status < 0 ? 0 : status;
   390	    }
   391	    checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(settings.data(), 3, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Bcast");
   392	    const size_t numOptions = static_cast<size_t>(settings[0]);
   393	    const bool validate = settings[1] != 0;
   394	    const bool printResults = settings[2] != 0;
   395	    const size_t collectCount = printResults ? numOptions : validate ? std::min(size_t{10}, numOptions) : 0;
   396	
   397	    MPI_Comm nodeComm;
   398	    checkMpi(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
   399	                                MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm), "MPI_Comm_split_type");
   400	    int nodeRank = 0, nodeRanks = 1;
   401	    checkMpi(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank), "MPI_Comm_rank");
   402	    checkMpi(MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeRanks), "MPI_Comm_size");
   403	    checkMpi(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm), "MPI_Comm_free");
   404	
   405	    int deviceCount = 0;
   406	    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
   407	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   408	        throw std::runtime_error("MPI/OpenMP/CUDA execution requires a visible CUDA device");
   409	    }
   410	    // Divide visible GPUs among node-local ranks. This also respects scheduler
   411	    // CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES masks, including one visible device per rank.
   412	    const int available = deviceCount >= nodeRanks
   413	                        ? 1 + (deviceCount - 1 - nodeRank) / nodeRanks : 1;
   414	    // Weight each rank by its GPU count, so uneven GPU counts per rank or
   415	    // node do not leave faster ranks waiting for ranks with fewer devices.
   416	    const unsigned long long localDevices = static_cast<unsigned long long>(available);
   417	    unsigned long long totalDevices = 0, firstDevice = 0;
   418	    checkMpi(MPI_Allreduce(&localDevices, &totalDevices, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   419	                          MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Allreduce");
   420	    checkMpi(MPI_Exscan(&localDevices, &firstDevice, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   421	                       MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Exscan");
   422	    if (rank == 0) {
   423	        firstDevice = 0;
   424	    }
   425	    if (totalDevices > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   426	        throw std::runtime_error("GPU count exceeds addressable range");
   427	    }
   428	    const Range localRange = partitionByDevices(numOptions, static_cast<size_t>(firstDevice),
   429	                                                static_cast<size_t>(localDevices),
   430	                                                static_cast<size_t>(totalDevices));
   431	    const int workerCount = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(available),
   432	                                                    std::max(size_t{1}, localRange.count)));
   433	    std::vector<GpuWorker> workers(workerCount);
   434	    for (int i = 0; i < workerCount; ++i) {
   435	        workers[i].deviceId = deviceCount >= nodeRanks ? nodeRank + i * nodeRanks
   436	                                                      : nodeRank % deviceCount;
   437	        workers[i].range = partition(localRange.count, i, workerCount);
   438	        workers[i].range.first += localRange.first;
   439	    }
   440	
   441	    // Root's local output goes directly into the final ordered result vector.
   442	    // Validation alone needs only the same first ten values as the original.
   443	    std::vector<double> results(rank == 0 ? collectCount : 0);
   444	    const size_t localCollectCount = localRange.first < collectCount
   445	                                  ? std::min(localRange.count, collectCount - localRange.first) : 0;
   446	    std::vector<double> localResults(rank != 0 ? localCollectCount : 0);
   447	    double* output = rank == 0 ? results.data() : localResults.data();
   448	
   449	    omp_set_dynamic(0);
   450	    const auto coefficients = prepareCoefficients();
   451	    onGpus(workers, rank, [&](GpuWorker& worker) { worker.initialize(coefficients, collectCount); });
   452	
   453	    if (rank == 0) {
   454	        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
   455	        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
   456	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   457	        printf("Pricing options...\n");
   458	    }
   459	    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
   460	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   461	    onGpus(workers, rank, [&](GpuWorker& worker) {
   462	        worker.price(output, localRange.first, collectCount);
   463	    });
   464	    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   465	    double duration = 0.0;
   466	    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Reduce");
   467	
   468	    gatherResults(collectCount, localRange, output, results, rank, ranks);
   469	    onGpus(workers, rank, [](GpuWorker& worker) { worker.release(); });
   470	
   471	    if (rank == 0) {
   472	        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
   473	        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
   474	        if (printResults) {
   475	            print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
   476	        }
   477	        if (validate) {
   478	            printf("Validating results...\n");
   479	            status = validateResults(results) ? 0 : 1;
   480	            printf("Validation: %s\n", status == 0 ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   481	        }
   482	    }
   483	    checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Bcast");
   484	    return status;
   485	}
   486	
   487	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   488	    int provided = 0;
   489	    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   490	        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI_Init_thread failed\n");
   491	        return 1;
   492	    }
   493	    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
   494	    int status = 1;
   495	    try {
   496	        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN), "MPI_Comm_set_errhandler");
   497	        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank), "MPI_Comm_rank");
   498	        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks), "MPI_Comm_size");
   499	        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   500	            throw std::runtime_error("MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support is required");
   501	        }
   502	        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
   503	    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   504	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
   505	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   506	        return 1;
   507	    }
   508	    if (MPI_Finalize() != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   509	        return 1;
   510	    }
   511	    return status;
   512	}

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }
 }

+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.

+    double drift;

+    double discount;

+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];

+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];

+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)
+        };
+    }
+    return table;

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = first + i;
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / 7.0);
+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}
+
+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(), sizeof(table)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }
 }

+        workers[i].deviceId = deviceCount >= nodeRanks ? nodeRank + i * nodeRanks
+                                                      : nodeRank % deviceCount;
+        workers[i].range = partition(localRange.count, i, workerCount);
+        workers[i].range.first += localRange.first;
+    }
+
+    // Root's local output goes directly into the final ordered result vector.
+    // Validation alone needs only the same first ten values as the original.
+    std::vector<double> results(rank == 0 ? collectCount : 0);
+    const size_t localCollectCount = localRange.first < collectCount
+                                  ? std::min(localRange.count, collectCount - localRange.first) : 0;
+    std::vector<double> localResults(rank != 0 ? localCollectCount : 0);
+    double* output = rank == 0 ? results.data() : localResults.data();
+
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const auto coefficients = prepareCoefficients();
+    onGpus(workers, rank, [&](GpuWorker& worker) { worker.initialize(coefficients, collectCount); });
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
+        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
+        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        printf("Pricing options...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    onGpus(workers, rank, [&](GpuWorker& worker) {
+        worker.price(output, localRange.first, collectCount);
+    });
+    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double duration = 0.0;
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Reduce");
+
+    gatherResults(collectCount, localRange, output, results, rank, ranks);
+    onGpus(workers, rank, [](GpuWorker& worker) { worker.release(); });
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
+        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
         }
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating results...\n");
+            status = validateResults(results) ? 0 : 1;
+            printf("Validation: %s\n", status == 0 ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+        }
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Bcast");
+    return status;

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;

+         i < count; i += stride) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}

+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}

+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Identify physical devices rather than comparing CUDA ordinals: schedulers
+// can expose identical, disjoint, reordered, or overlapping visibility masks.
+// Give each physical GPU exactly one node-local rank, avoiding oversubscription.
+std::vector<int> assignDevices(MPI_Comm nodeComm, int nodeRank, int nodeRanks, int deviceCount) {
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> localIds(deviceCount);
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, i), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        localIds[i] = properties.uuid;
     }
-    
-    return price;
+    const int localBytes = deviceCount * static_cast<int>(sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    std::vector<int> counts(nodeRanks), displacements(nodeRanks);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgather(&localBytes, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, nodeComm),
+             "MPI_Allgather");
+    int totalBytes = 0;
+    for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+        if (counts[r] > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() - totalBytes) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Node GPU metadata exceeds MPI count limit");
+        }
+        displacements[r] = totalBytes;
+        totalBytes += counts[r];
+    }
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> allIds(static_cast<size_t>(totalBytes) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgatherv(localIds.data(), localBytes, MPI_BYTE, allIds.data(), counts.data(),
+                           displacements.data(), MPI_BYTE, nodeComm), "MPI_Allgatherv");
+    const auto sameDevice = [](const cudaUUID_t& a, const cudaUUID_t& b) {
+        return std::memcmp(a.bytes, b.bytes, sizeof(a.bytes)) == 0;
+    };
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> uniqueIds;
+    for (const auto& id : allIds) {
+        if (std::none_of(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+                         [&](const cudaUUID_t& other) { return sameDevice(id, other); })) {
+            uniqueIds.push_back(id);
+        }
+    }
+    std::vector<int> devices;
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        const auto position = std::find_if(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+            [&](const cudaUUID_t& id) { return sameDevice(id, localIds[i]); });
+        const size_t ordinal = static_cast<size_t>(position - uniqueIds.begin());
+        std::vector<int> candidates;
+        for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+            const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            const size_t end = begin + static_cast<size_t>(counts[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+                if (sameDevice(localIds[i], allIds[j])) {
+                    candidates.push_back(r);
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (candidates[ordinal % candidates.size()] == nodeRank) {
+            devices.push_back(i);
+        }
+    }
+    return devices;
 }

+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(),
+                                     table.size() * sizeof(PricingCoefficients)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    if (workers.empty()) {
+        return;
+    }
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }

+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }

+// These quantities are invariant under the original spot/strike scaling.
+// Hoist their expensive sqrt/exp operations out of the per-option kernel.
+struct PricingCoefficients {
+    int type;
+    double strike;
+    double spot;
+    double drift;
+    double volatilityTime;
+    double dividendDiscount;
+    double discount;
+};

+using CoefficientTable = std::array<PricingCoefficients, 7>;
+__constant__ PricingCoefficients deviceOptions[7];

+CoefficientTable prepareCoefficients() {
+    CoefficientTable table{};
+    constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+        const auto& option = options[i];
+        table[i] = {
+            option.type, option.strike, option.spot,
+            (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+            option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+            exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t)
+        };
+    }
+    return table;

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {

+        prices[i] = blackScholes(deviceOptions[globalIndex % 7], factor);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }

+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }

+struct Range {
+    size_t first;

+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}
+
+// Identify physical devices rather than comparing CUDA ordinals: schedulers
+// can expose identical, disjoint, reordered, or overlapping visibility masks.
+// Give each physical GPU exactly one node-local rank, avoiding oversubscription.
+std::vector<int> assignDevices(MPI_Comm nodeComm, int nodeRank, int nodeRanks, int deviceCount) {
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> localIds(deviceCount);
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, i), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        localIds[i] = properties.uuid;
     }
-    
-    return price;
+    const int localBytes = deviceCount * static_cast<int>(sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    std::vector<int> counts(nodeRanks), displacements(nodeRanks);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgather(&localBytes, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, nodeComm),
+             "MPI_Allgather");
+    int totalBytes = 0;
+    for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+        if (counts[r] > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() - totalBytes) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Node GPU metadata exceeds MPI count limit");
+        }
+        displacements[r] = totalBytes;
+        totalBytes += counts[r];
+    }
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> allIds(static_cast<size_t>(totalBytes) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgatherv(localIds.data(), localBytes, MPI_BYTE, allIds.data(), counts.data(),
+                           displacements.data(), MPI_BYTE, nodeComm), "MPI_Allgatherv");
+    const auto sameDevice = [](const cudaUUID_t& a, const cudaUUID_t& b) {
+        return std::memcmp(a.bytes, b.bytes, sizeof(a.bytes)) == 0;
+    };
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> uniqueIds;
+    for (const auto& id : allIds) {
+        if (std::none_of(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+                         [&](const cudaUUID_t& other) { return sameDevice(id, other); })) {
+            uniqueIds.push_back(id);
+        }
+    }
+    std::vector<int> devices;
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        const auto position = std::find_if(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+            [&](const cudaUUID_t& id) { return sameDevice(id, localIds[i]); });
+        const size_t ordinal = static_cast<size_t>(position - uniqueIds.begin());
+        std::vector<int> candidates;
+        for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+            const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            const size_t end = begin + static_cast<size_t>(counts[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+                if (sameDevice(localIds[i], allIds[j])) {
+                    candidates.push_back(r);
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (candidates[ordinal % candidates.size()] == nodeRank) {
+            devices.push_back(i);
+        }
+    }
+    return devices;
 }

+// Each OpenMP worker owns one GPU and two bounded buffers. Generation and
+// pricing are fused; only requested output is copied back. Pinned staging
+// buffers let the next kernel overlap a device-to-host transfer and host copy.
+struct GpuWorker {
+    static constexpr size_t chunkOptions = 1u << 20;
+    static constexpr int blockSize = 256;
+
+    struct Buffer {
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* device = nullptr;
+        double* host = nullptr;
+        size_t hostOffset = 0;
+        size_t copyCount = 0;
+        bool pending = false;
+    };
+
+    int deviceId = 0;
+    Range range{};
+    size_t capacity = 0;
+    int slots = 0;
+    int maxBlocks = 0;
+    std::array<Buffer, 2> buffers{};
+
+    void initialize(const CoefficientTable& table, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        if (range.count == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, deviceId), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = properties.multiProcessorCount * 32;
+        capacity = std::min(chunkOptions, range.count);
+        slots = range.count > capacity ? 2 : 1;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, table.data(),
+                                     table.size() * sizeof(PricingCoefficients)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buffer.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.device),
+                                 capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc");
+            if (range.first < collectCount) {
+                checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buffer.host),
+                                         capacity * sizeof(double)), "cudaMallocHost");
+            }
+        }
+        // Initialize the kernel/module outside the measured pricing interval.
+        priceOptions<<<1, 1, 0, buffers[0].stream>>>(buffers[0].device, range.first, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions warmup");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffers[0].stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+    }
+
+    void finish(Buffer& buffer, double* output) {
+        if (buffer.pending) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(buffer.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                std::memcpy(output + buffer.hostOffset, buffer.host,
+                            buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double));
+            }
+            buffer.pending = false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(double* output, size_t rankFirst, size_t collectCount) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        int slot = 0;
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < range.count;) {
+            auto& buffer = buffers[slot];
+            finish(buffer, output);
+            const size_t count = std::min(capacity, range.count - offset);
+            const size_t first = range.first + offset;
+            const int blocks = std::min(maxBlocks,
+                                       static_cast<int>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, buffer.stream>>>(buffer.device, first, count);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions");
+            buffer.copyCount = first < collectCount ? std::min(count, collectCount - first) : 0;
+            if (buffer.copyCount != 0) {
+                buffer.hostOffset = first - rankFirst;
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(buffer.host, buffer.device,
+                                          buffer.copyCount * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, buffer.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            }
+            buffer.pending = true;
+            offset += count;
+            slot = (slot + 1) % slots;
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            finish(buffers[i], output);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(deviceId), "cudaSetDevice");
+        for (int i = 0; i < slots; ++i) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFree(buffers[i].device), "cudaFree");
+            if (buffers[i].host != nullptr) {
+                checkCuda(cudaFreeHost(buffers[i].host), "cudaFreeHost");
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(buffers[i].stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename Function>
+void onGpus(std::vector<GpuWorker>& workers, int rank, Function action) {
+    if (workers.empty()) {
+        return;
+    }
+    int failed = 0;
+    const int count = static_cast<int>(workers.size());
+    // A parallel for still visits every device if OMP_THREAD_LIMIT reduces
+    // the team size. MPI calls are confined to the main thread after this.
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static, 1) reduction(|:failed)
+    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        try {
+            action(workers[i]);
+        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+            #pragma omp critical
+            {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d, CUDA device %d: %s\n",
+                             rank, workers[i].deviceId, error.what());
+            }
+            failed = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA worker failed");
+    }
 }

+// Gather in globally ordered windows so MPI's int counts/displacements never
+// overflow, even for more than INT_MAX options. No communication of inputs,
+// and no result gather when neither -r nor -v requests it.
+void gatherResults(size_t collectCount, Range localRange,
+                   const double* localResults, std::vector<double>& results,
+                   int rank, int ranks) {
+    if (collectCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::array<unsigned long long, 2> localMetadata{{localRange.first, localRange.count}};
+    std::vector<unsigned long long> rankRanges(rank == 0 ? size_t{2} * ranks : 0);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Gather(localMetadata.data(), 2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, rankRanges.data(),
+                        2, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Gather");
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < collectCount;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, collectCount - first);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t index = size_t{2} * r;
+                const Range range{static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index]),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(rankRanges[index + 1])};
+                const size_t begin = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t finish = std::min(end, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[r] = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+                displacements[r] = counts[r] != 0 ? static_cast<int>(begin - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        const size_t begin = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t finish = std::min(end, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int count = finish > begin ? static_cast<int>(finish - begin) : 0;
+        const double* send = count != 0 ? localResults + (begin - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : send, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                            counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv");
+        first = end;
     }
 }

+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ prices, size_t first, size_t count) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}

+void checkMpi(int error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;
+        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::string(message, length));
+    }
+}

+struct Range {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Range partition(size_t count, int index, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = count / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = count % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return {base * static_cast<size_t>(index) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+Range partitionByDevices(size_t count, size_t firstDevice, size_t devices, size_t totalDevices) {
+    const size_t base = count / totalDevices;
+    const size_t remainder = count % totalDevices;
+    const size_t first = base * firstDevice + std::min(firstDevice, remainder);
+    const size_t end = base * (firstDevice + devices) + std::min(firstDevice + devices, remainder);
+    return {first, end - first};
+}

+// Identify physical devices rather than comparing CUDA ordinals: schedulers
+// can expose identical, disjoint, reordered, or overlapping visibility masks.
+// Give each physical GPU exactly one node-local rank, avoiding oversubscription.
+std::vector<int> assignDevices(MPI_Comm nodeComm, int nodeRank, int nodeRanks, int deviceCount) {
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> localIds(deviceCount);
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, i), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        localIds[i] = properties.uuid;
     }
-    
-    return price;
+    const int localBytes = deviceCount * static_cast<int>(sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    std::vector<int> counts(nodeRanks), displacements(nodeRanks);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgather(&localBytes, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, nodeComm),
+             "MPI_Allgather");
+    int totalBytes = 0;
+    for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+        if (counts[r] > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() - totalBytes) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Node GPU metadata exceeds MPI count limit");
+        }
+        displacements[r] = totalBytes;
+        totalBytes += counts[r];
+    }
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> allIds(static_cast<size_t>(totalBytes) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t));
+    checkMpi(MPI_Allgatherv(localIds.data(), localBytes, MPI_BYTE, allIds.data(), counts.data(),
+                           displacements.data(), MPI_BYTE, nodeComm), "MPI_Allgatherv");
+    const auto sameDevice = [](const cudaUUID_t& a, const cudaUUID_t& b) {
+        return std::memcmp(a.bytes, b.bytes, sizeof(a.bytes)) == 0;
+    };
+    std::vector<cudaUUID_t> uniqueIds;
+    for (const auto& id : allIds) {
+        if (std::none_of(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+                         [&](const cudaUUID_t& other) { return sameDevice(id, other); })) {
+            uniqueIds.push_back(id);
+        }
+    }
+    std::vector<int> devices;
+    for (int i = 0; i < deviceCount; ++i) {
+        const auto position = std::find_if(uniqueIds.begin(), uniqueIds.end(),
+            [&](const cudaUUID_t& id) { return sameDevice(id, localIds[i]); });
+        const size_t ordinal = static_cast<size_t>(position - uniqueIds.begin());
+        std::vector<int> candidates;
+        for (int r = 0; r < nodeRanks; ++r) {
+            const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            const size_t end = begin + static_cast<size_t>(counts[r]) / sizeof(cudaUUID_t);
+            for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+                if (sameDevice(localIds[i], allIds[j])) {
+                    candidates.push_back(r);
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (candidates[ordinal % candidates.size()] == nodeRank) {
+            devices.push_back(i);
+        }
+    }
+    return devices;
 }
